@FiniteThreeLocks.H3L_isSelfAdjoint : ∀ {n : ℕ} (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)),
  IsSelfAdjoint (FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz)
@FiniteThreeLocks.H3L_quadratic_form : ∀ {n : ℕ} (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n))
  (x : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)),
  RCLike.re (inner ℂ x ((FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz) x)) = ‖Dc x‖ ^ 2 + ‖Db x‖ ^ 2 + ‖Dz x‖ ^ 2

  (x : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)), 0 ≤ RCLike.re (inner ℂ x ((FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz) x))
@FiniteThreeLocks.ker_H3L_eq_threeLocks : ∀ {n : ℕ}
  (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)),
  (FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz).ker = Dc.ker ⊓ Db.ker ⊓ Dz.ker

  IsIdempotentElem (FiniteThreeLocks.PF Dc Db Dz)
@FiniteThreeLocks.PF_isSelfAdjoint : ∀ {n : ℕ} (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n))
  (u v : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)),
  inner ℂ ((FiniteThreeLocks.PF Dc Db Dz) u) v = inner ℂ u ((FiniteThreeLocks.PF Dc Db Dz) v)
@FiniteThreeLocks.PF_apply_mem : ∀ {n : ℕ} (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n))
  (x : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)), (FiniteThreeLocks.PF Dc Db Dz) x ∈ (FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz).ker
@FiniteThreeLocks.PF_eq_self_iff : ∀ {n : ℕ} (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n))
  (v : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)), (FiniteThreeLocks.PF Dc Db Dz) v = v ↔ v ∈ (FiniteThreeLocks.H3L Dc Db Dz).ker
@FiniteThreeLocks.normalizedCornerTrace_PF : ∀ {n : ℕ}

  0 < FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz →
    ↑(FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz) / ↑(FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz) = 1
@FiniteThreeLocks.equalConjugateFaces_halfTrace : ∀ {n : ℕ}
  (Dc Db Dz : EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n) →ₗ[ℂ] EuclideanSpace ℂ (Fin n)) (dplus dminus : ℕ),
  dplus + dminus = FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz →
    dplus = dminus → 0 < FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz → ↑dplus / ↑(FiniteThreeLocks.cornerDim Dc Db Dz) = 1 / 2

  P * P = P →
    U * U = P →
      P * U = U → U * P = U → TransportData.facePlus P U * TransportData.facePlus P U = TransportData.facePlus P U

@NameRelation.tl3_linearly_independent : ∀ {b : ℝ},
  0 < b →
    b < 1 →
      ∀ (c0 c1 c2 c3 c4 : ℝ),
        c0 • 1 + c1 • NameRelation.E1 + c2 • NameRelation.E2 b + c3 • (NameRelation.E1 * NameRelation.E2 b) +
              c4 • (NameRelation.E2 b * NameRelation.E1) =
            0 →
          c0 = 0 ∧ c1 = 0 ∧ c2 = 0 ∧ c3 = 0 ∧ c4 = 0

@CoreSupport.support_annihilates : ∀ {A : Type} [inst : Ring A] [StarRing A] [Algebra ℂ A] (q : A),
  q * q = q → q * (1 - q) = 0
@CoreSupport.support_maximal : ∀ {A : Type} [inst : Ring A] [StarRing A] [Algebra ℂ A] (q r : A),
  r * (1 - q) = 0 → r * q = r
@CoreSupport.threeLocksFromSupport : {W : SpecificAQFT.TGLSpecificAQFTWitness} →
  {D : ModularRealization.WedgeModularData W} →
    (C : ModularRealization.ContinuousCoreData W D) →
      (q qp qm : C.Core) →
        q * q = q →
          star q = q →
            q ≠ 0 →
              C.canonicalTrace q = 1 →
                qp * qp = qp →
                  star qp = qp →
                    qm * qm = qm →
                      star qm = qm →
                        qp + qm = q →
                          qp * qm = 0 →
                            C.canonicalTrace q = C.canonicalTrace qp + C.canonicalTrace qm →
                              C.canonicalTrace qp = C.canonicalTrace qm → ModularRealization.ThreeLocksCoreData W D C
@CoreSupport.realizationFromSupport : {W : SpecificAQFT.TGLSpecificAQFTWitness} →
  ¬FiniteDimensional ℂ W.H →
    (D : ModularRealization.WedgeModularData W) →
      (C : ModularRealization.ContinuousCoreData W D) →
        (q qp qm : C.Core) →
          q * q = q →
            star q = q →
              q ≠ 0 →
                C.canonicalTrace q = 1 →
                  qp * qp = qp →
                    star qp = qp →
                      qm * qm = qm →
                        star qm = qm →
                          qp + qm = q →
                            qp * qm = 0 →
                              C.canonicalTrace q = C.canonicalTrace qp + C.canonicalTrace qm →
                                C.canonicalTrace qp = C.canonicalTrace qm → ModularRealization.TGLModularRealization W

  [inst_2 : Algebra ℂ N] [inst_3 : Ring M] [inst_4 : StarRing M] [inst_5 : Algebra ℂ M] [inst_6 : Ring Ext]
  [inst_7 : StarRing Ext] [inst_8 : Algebra ℂ Ext] {T1 T2 : TransportData.JonesTowerData N M Ext},
  CoreSupport.nameGaugeEquiv T1 T2 →
    TransportData.transportDefect T1.upper T1.eJones = TransportData.transportDefect T2.upper T2.eJones

  TGLExt.Cmat (∑ p, TGLExt.Lmul (D p.1 p.2) * TGLExt.Rmul (Matrix.single p.1 p.2 1)) k₀ l₀ = D k₀ l₀

  {Φ : Module.End ℂ (Matrix n n ℂ)},
  Φ ∈ TGLExt.commutantSet (TGLExt.Lmul '' A) →
    ∀ {a : Matrix n n ℂ}, a ∈ A → ∀ (k l : n), a * TGLExt.Cmat Φ k l = TGLExt.Cmat Φ k l * a

  {Φ : Module.End ℂ (Matrix n n ℂ)},
  Φ ∈ TGLExt.commutantSet (TGLExt.Lmul '' A) →
    Φ ∈ Submodule.span ℂ {f | ∃ x y, (∀ a ∈ A, a * x = x * a) ∧ f = TGLExt.Lmul x * TGLExt.Rmul y}

      ∀ {a : Matrix n n ℂ},
        a ∈ A →
          TGLExt.Lmul a * TGLExt.frobProj (Submodule.span ℂ A) = TGLExt.frobProj (Submodule.span ℂ A) * TGLExt.Lmul a

  TGLExt.Smat θ =
    TGLExt.Umat * Matrix.diagonal ![Complex.exp (↑θ * Complex.I), Complex.exp (-(↑θ * Complex.I))] * TGLExt.Uinv

@TGLExt.cocycle_chain : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (phi psi chi : Matrix n n ℂ) (t : ℝ),
  TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t * TGLExt.cocycle psi chi t = TGLExt.cocycle phi chi t

  (t : ℝ), TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t * TGLExt.cocycle psi chi t * TGLExt.cocycle chi phi t = 1

  TGLExt.cocycle phi psi (s + t) = TGLExt.cocycle phi psi s * TGLExt.sigma psi s (TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t)

  (t : ℝ), (TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t).conjTranspose = TGLExt.cocycle psi phi t

  TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t ∈ unitary (Matrix n n ℂ)

  Commute (TGLExt.logRho phi) (TGLExt.logRho psi) →
    ∀ (t : ℝ), TGLExt.cocycle phi psi t = NormedSpace.exp ((↑t * Complex.I) • (TGLExt.logRho phi - TGLExt.logRho psi))

      ∀ (x : Matrix n n ℂ), (∀ (t : ℝ), TGLExt.sigma (TGLExt.rhoD d) t x = x) ↔ x = TGLExt.diagExpect x

  (∀ (i : n), g i i = 0) →

  {n : Type} [inst_3 : Fintype n] [inst_4 : DecidableEq n] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) {φ ψ : Matrix n n ℂ},

    ψ.PosDef →
      ∀ (g : G) (t : ℝ),

  TGLExt.Dchi chi * TGLExt.lam g = chi g • (TGLExt.lam g * TGLExt.Dchi chi)

  [inst_3 : Fintype n] [inst_4 : DecidableEq n] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) (chi : G →* ℂ) (a : Matrix n n ℂ),
  TGLExt.Dchi chi * TGLExt.piRep u a = TGLExt.piRep u a * TGLExt.Dchi chi

  [inst_3 : Fintype n] [inst_4 : DecidableEq n] (chi : G →* ℂ),
  (∀ (g : G), star (chi g) * chi g = 1) →
    ∀ (g : G), TGLExt.Dchi chi * TGLExt.lam g * (TGLExt.Dchi chi).conjTranspose = chi g • TGLExt.lam g

  [inst_3 : Fintype n] [inst_4 : DecidableEq n] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) (chi : G →* ℂ) {ρ : Matrix n n ℂ},
  ρ.PosDef →
    ∀ (t : ℝ),
      TGLExt.Dchi chi * TGLExt.modPow (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) t = TGLExt.modPow (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) t * TGLExt.Dchi chi
@TGLExt.gibbs_Dchi : ∀ {G : Type} [inst : Group G] [inst_1 : Fintype G] [inst_2 : DecidableEq G] {n : Type}
  [inst_3 : Fintype n] [inst_4 : DecidableEq n] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) (chi : G →* ℂ),
  (∀ (g : G), star (chi g) * chi g = 1) →
    ∀ (ρ : Matrix n n ℂ) (x : Matrix (G × n) (G × n) ℂ),
      TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) (TGLExt.Dchi chi * x * (TGLExt.Dchi chi).conjTranspose) =
        TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) x
TGLExt.dyadic_approx : ∀ (t : ℝ) (n : ℕ), |t - TGLExt.dyadicNearest t n| ≤ (2 ^ n)⁻¹ / 2
TGLExt.dyadic_stage_mono : ∀ (k : ℤ) (n : ℕ), ↑k / 2 ^ n = ↑(2 * k) / 2 ^ (n + 1)
TGLExt.dyadic_tendsto : ∀ (t : ℝ), Filter.Tendsto (fun n => TGLExt.dyadicNearest t n) Filter.atTop (nhds t)
TGLExt.annihilator_fixes_stage : ∀ (n : ℕ) (k : ℤ),
  Complex.exp (-Complex.I * (2 * ↑Real.pi * 2 ^ n) * (↑k * (2 ^ n)⁻¹)) = 1
@TGLExt.scaling_fixed_eq_zero : ∀ {c x : ℝ}, c ≠ 1 → x = c * x → x = 0
@TGLExt.DualScalingData.fixed_tau_zero : ∀ {M : Type} (D : TGLExt.DualScalingData M) {s₀ : ℝ},
  0 < s₀ → ∀ {m : M}, D.theta s₀ m = m → D.tau m = 0
@TGLExt.DualScalingData.dyadic_stage_tau_zero : ∀ {M : Type} (D : TGLExt.DualScalingData M) (n : ℕ) {m : M},
  D.theta (2 * Real.pi * 2 ^ n) m = m → D.tau m = 0
TGLExt.sFrame_zero : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (g : Fin 2 → Fin 2 → ℝ) (x : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ), TGLExt.sFrame θ g 0 x = x

  TGLExt.sFrame θ g (s + t) x = TGLExt.sFrame θ g s (TGLExt.sFrame θ g t x)
TGLExt.sFrame_tendsto : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (g : Fin 2 → Fin 2 → ℝ),
  (∀ (i : Fin 2), g i i = 0) →
    (∀ (i j : Fin 2), i ≠ j → 0 < g i j) →
      ∀ (x : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ),
        Filter.Tendsto (fun t => TGLExt.sFrame θ g t x) Filter.atTop
          (nhds
            ((TGLExt.Smat θ).conjTranspose * TGLExt.diagExpect (TGLExt.Smat θ * x * (TGLExt.Smat θ).conjTranspose) *
              TGLExt.Smat θ))
TGLExt.dephased_rhoOut_zero_zero : ∀ (θ : ℝ), TGLExt.diagExpect (TGLExt.rhoOut θ) 0 0 = ↑(Real.cos θ) ^ 2
TGLExt.dephased_rhoOut_one_one : ∀ (θ : ℝ), TGLExt.diagExpect (TGLExt.rhoOut θ) 1 1 = ↑(Real.sin θ) ^ 2

  (∀ (i : Fin 2), g i i = 0) →
    (∀ (i j : Fin 2), i ≠ j → 0 < g i j) →
      Filter.Tendsto (fun t => TGLExt.dephase g t (TGLExt.rhoOut θ)) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (TGLExt.diagExpect (TGLExt.rhoOut θ)))

  Commute ρ H → ∀ (f : ℝ → ℝ) (t : ℝ), TGLExt.sigma ρ t (cfc f H) = cfc f H

  0 < s₀ → ∀ {m : M}, D.tau m ≠ 0 → D.theta s₀ m ≠ m
@TGLExt.cornerProj_apply : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [inst : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G] [Fintype G]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq G] (S : Finset G) (p q : G × n),
  TGLExt.cornerProj S p q = if p = q then if p.1 ∈ S then 1 else 0 else 0
@TGLExt.cornerProj_idem : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (S : Finset G),
  TGLExt.cornerProj S * TGLExt.cornerProj S = TGLExt.cornerProj S
@TGLExt.cornerProj_conjTranspose : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [inst : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G] [Fintype G]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq G] (S : Finset G), (TGLExt.cornerProj S).conjTranspose = TGLExt.cornerProj S
@TGLExt.cornerProj_mono : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] {S₁ S₂ : Finset G},
  S₁ ⊆ S₂ → TGLExt.cornerProj S₁ * TGLExt.cornerProj S₂ = TGLExt.cornerProj S₁
@TGLExt.lam_conj_cornerProj : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [inst_2 : Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (g : G) (S : Finset G),
  TGLExt.lam g * TGLExt.cornerProj S * (TGLExt.lam g).conjTranspose =
    TGLExt.cornerProj (Finset.image (fun a => g * a) S)
@TGLExt.trace_cornerProj : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (S : Finset G), (TGLExt.cornerProj S).trace = ↑(S.card * Fintype.card n)
@TGLExt.cornerProj_comm_piRep : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [inst_2 : Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) (S : Finset G) (a : Matrix n n ℂ),
  TGLExt.cornerProj S * TGLExt.piRep u a = TGLExt.piRep u a * TGLExt.cornerProj S
@TGLExt.cornerProj_comm_modPow : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [inst_2 : Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) {ρ : Matrix n n ℂ},
  ρ.PosDef →
    ∀ (S : Finset G) (t : ℝ),
      TGLExt.cornerProj S * TGLExt.modPow (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) t =
        TGLExt.modPow (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) t * TGLExt.cornerProj S

  [inst_3 : DecidableEq G], TGLExt.cornerProj Finset.univ = 1
TGLExt.boost_zero : TGLExt.boost 0 = 1
TGLExt.boost_add : ∀ (s t : ℝ), TGLExt.boost s * TGLExt.boost t = TGLExt.boost (s + t)
TGLExt.boost_preserves_eta : ∀ (s : ℝ), (TGLExt.boost s).transpose * TGLExt.minkEta * TGLExt.boost s = TGLExt.minkEta
TGLExt.boost_det : ∀ (s : ℝ), (TGLExt.boost s).det = 1

  |t| < x → |(TGLExt.boost s).mulVec ![x, t] 1| < (TGLExt.boost s).mulVec ![x, t] 0
@TGLExt.logRho_gibbs_boost : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (K : Matrix n n ℂ),
  IsSelfAdjoint K → TGLExt.logRho (NormedSpace.exp (-(2 * Real.pi) • K)) = -(2 * Real.pi) • K
@TGLExt.modPow_gibbs_boost : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (K : Matrix n n ℂ),
  IsSelfAdjoint K →
    ∀ (t : ℝ),
      TGLExt.modPow (NormedSpace.exp (-(2 * Real.pi) • K)) t = NormedSpace.exp ((↑(-(2 * Real.pi) * t) * Complex.I) • K)
@TGLExt.sigma_gibbs_boost : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (K : Matrix n n ℂ),
  IsSelfAdjoint K →
    ∀ (t : ℝ) (a : Matrix n n ℂ),
      TGLExt.sigma (NormedSpace.exp (-(2 * Real.pi) • K)) t a =
        NormedSpace.exp ((↑(-(2 * Real.pi) * t) * Complex.I) • K) * a *
          NormedSpace.exp ((↑(2 * Real.pi * t) * Complex.I) • K)

TGLExt.polCross_symm : TGLExt.polCross.transpose = TGLExt.polCross

TGLExt.polCross_traceless : TGLExt.polCross.trace = 0
TGLExt.polarization_decomposition : ∀ (h : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ),
  h.transpose = h → h.trace = 0 → h = h 0 0 • TGLExt.polPlus + h 0 1 • TGLExt.polCross
TGLExt.polarizations_independent : ∀ (a b : ℝ), a • TGLExt.polPlus + b • TGLExt.polCross = 0 → a = 0 ∧ b = 0
TGLExt.rot_add : ∀ (a b : ℝ), TGLExt.rot a * TGLExt.rot b = TGLExt.rot (a + b)

  (TGLExt.rot θ).transpose * TGLExt.polPlus * TGLExt.rot θ =
    Real.cos (2 * θ) • TGLExt.polPlus + Real.sin (2 * θ) • TGLExt.polCross
TGLExt.rot_conj_polCross : ∀ (θ : ℝ),
  (TGLExt.rot θ).transpose * TGLExt.polCross * TGLExt.rot θ =
    -Real.sin (2 * θ) • TGLExt.polPlus + Real.cos (2 * θ) • TGLExt.polCross

  i = 1 ∨ i = 2 → j = 1 ∨ j = 2 → TGLExt.gaugeSym TGLExt.nullK ξ i j = 0

@TGLExt.variance_of_projection : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (ρ P : Matrix n n ℂ),
  P * P = P → TGLExt.gibbs ρ (P * P) - TGLExt.gibbs ρ P ^ 2 = TGLExt.gibbs ρ P * (1 - TGLExt.gibbs ρ P)

    ↑(Real.sin θ) ^ 2 * ↑(Real.cos θ) ^ 2

  U ∈ unitary (Matrix n n ℂ) → TGLExt.purity (U * x * U.conjTranspose) = TGLExt.purity x

  TGLExt.gibbs (ρ₁ + ρ₂) a = TGLExt.gibbs ρ₁ a + TGLExt.gibbs ρ₂ a

@TGLExt.zero_mode_state_minimizes : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] {H : Matrix n n ℂ},
  H.PosSemidef → ∀ {v : n → ℂ}, H.mulVec v = 0 → (∀ (w : n → ℂ), 0 ≤ star w ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec w) ∧ star v ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec v = 0
@TGLExt.gibbs_is_critical : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (p q h : n → ℝ),
  (∀ (i : n), 0 < p i) →
    ∑ i, q i = 0 →
      ∀ (c : ℝ),
        (∀ (i : n), Real.log (p i) + h i = c) →
          HasDerivAt (fun ε => -∑ i, (p i + ε * q i) * Real.log (p i + ε * q i) - ∑ i, (p i + ε * q i) * h i) 0 0

  (∀ (i j : n), -Real.log (p i) - h i - (-Real.log (p j) - h j) = 0) → ∃ c, ∀ (i : n), Real.log (p i) + h i = c

  star v ⬝ᵥ (D.conjTranspose * D).mulVec v = star (D.mulVec v) ⬝ᵥ D.mulVec v

  star v ⬝ᵥ (Dc.conjTranspose * Dc + Db.conjTranspose * Db + Dz.conjTranspose * Dz).mulVec v = 0 ↔
    Dc.mulVec v = 0 ∧ Db.mulVec v = 0 ∧ Dz.mulVec v = 0

  H.IsHermitian → ∀ (v η : n → ℂ), (star v ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec η).re = (star η ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec v).re

  H.IsHermitian →
    ∀ (v η : n → ℂ),
      HasDerivAt (fun ε => (star (v + ↑ε • η) ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec (v + ↑ε • η)).re) (2 * (star η ⬝ᵥ H.mulVec v).re) 0

  (a : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.transport ρ t t a = a

  (a : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.transport ρ t r (TGLExt.transport ρ r s a) = TGLExt.transport ρ t s a

  Commute ρ H → ∀ (f : ℝ → ℝ) (t s : ℝ), TGLExt.transport ρ t s (cfc f H) = cfc f H

  [Fintype n] → [DecidableEq n] → Matrix n n ℂ → TGLExt.NamedTransportData (Matrix n n ℂ)
@TGLExt.canonicalNamedTransport_exists : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (ρ : Matrix n n ℂ),
  Nonempty (TGLExt.NamedTransportData (Matrix n n ℂ))
@TGLExt.excite_holonomy : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (A B x : Matrix n n ℂ),
  TGLExt.excite A (TGLExt.excite B x) - TGLExt.excite B (TGLExt.excite A x) = TGLExt.excite (TGLExt.excite A B) x
@TGLExt.excite_holonomy_flat : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (A B : Matrix n n ℂ),
  Commute A B → ∀ (x : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.excite A (TGLExt.excite B x) - TGLExt.excite B (TGLExt.excite A x) = 0
@TGLExt.trace_cornerProj_pos : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] [Nonempty n] {S : Finset G},
  S.Nonempty → 0 < (TGLExt.cornerProj S).trace
@TGLExt.cornerProj_loewner_mono : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [inst : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G] [Fintype G]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq G] {S₁ S₂ : Finset G}, S₁ ⊆ S₂ → TGLExt.cornerProj S₁ ≤ TGLExt.cornerProj S₂
@TGLExt.sigma_fixes_cornerProj : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [inst_2 : Group G]
  [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) {ρ : Matrix n n ℂ},
  ρ.PosDef → ∀ (S : Finset G) (t : ℝ), TGLExt.sigma (TGLExt.piRep u ρ) t (TGLExt.cornerProj S) = TGLExt.cornerProj S
@TGLExt.cornerProj_ne_of_ne : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [inst : DecidableEq n] {G : Type} [Group G] [Fintype G]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq G] [Nonempty n] {S₁ S₂ : Finset G}, S₁ ≠ S₂ → TGLExt.cornerProj S₁ ≠ TGLExt.cornerProj S₂
@TGLExt.TransportedCornerFamily : {n : Type} →
  [inst : Fintype n] →
    [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] →
      {G : Type} →
        [inst_2 : Group G] → [Fintype G] → [DecidableEq G] → (G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) → Matrix n n ℂ → Type
@TGLExt.canonicalTransportedCorner : {n : Type} →
  [inst : Fintype n] →
    [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] →
      {G : Type} →
        [inst_2 : Group G] →
          [inst_3 : Fintype G] →
            [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] →
              (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) → {ρ : Matrix n n ℂ} → ρ.PosDef → TGLExt.TransportedCornerFamily u ρ
@TGLExt.canonicalTransportedCorner_exists : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] {G : Type}
  [inst_2 : Group G] [inst_3 : Fintype G] [inst_4 : DecidableEq G] (u : G →* ↥(Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ))
  {ρ : Matrix n n ℂ}, ρ.PosDef → Nonempty (TGLExt.TransportedCornerFamily u ρ)
@TGLExt.ker_map_of_intertwine : ∀ {H₁ H₂ W₁ W₂ : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H₁] [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₁]
  [CompleteSpace H₁] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup H₂] [inst_4 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₂] [CompleteSpace H₂]
  [inst_6 : NormedAddCommGroup W₁] [inst_7 : NormedSpace ℂ W₁] [inst_8 : NormedAddCommGroup W₂]
  [inst_9 : NormedSpace ℂ W₂] (U : H₁ ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] H₂) (D₁ : H₁ →L[ℂ] W₁) (D₂ : H₂ →L[ℂ] W₂) (V : W₁ →ₗᵢ[ℂ] W₂),
  (∀ (x : H₁), D₂ (U x) = V (D₁ x)) → Submodule.map (↑U.toLinearEquiv) (↑D₁).ker = (↑D₂).ker
@TGLExt.starProjection_ker_covariant : ∀ {H₁ H₂ W₁ W₂ : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H₁]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₁] [inst_2 : CompleteSpace H₁] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup H₂]
  [inst_4 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₂] [inst_5 : CompleteSpace H₂] [inst_6 : NormedAddCommGroup W₁]
  [inst_7 : NormedSpace ℂ W₁] [inst_8 : NormedAddCommGroup W₂] [inst_9 : NormedSpace ℂ W₂] (U : H₁ ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] H₂)
  (D₁ : H₁ →L[ℂ] W₁) (D₂ : H₂ →L[ℂ] W₂) (V : W₁ →ₗᵢ[ℂ] W₂),
  (∀ (x : H₁), D₂ (U x) = V (D₁ x)) → ∀ (y : H₂), (↑D₂).ker.starProjection y = U ((↑D₁).ker.starProjection (U.symm y))
@TGLExt.starProjection_ker_internal_fix : ∀ {H₁ W₁ : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H₁]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₁] [inst_2 : CompleteSpace H₁] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup W₁]
  [inst_4 : NormedSpace ℂ W₁] (U : H₁ ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] H₁) (D : H₁ →L[ℂ] W₁) (V : W₁ →ₗᵢ[ℂ] W₁),
  (∀ (x : H₁), D (U x) = V (D x)) → ∀ (x : H₁), (↑D).ker.starProjection (U x) = U ((↑D).ker.starProjection x)
@TGLExt.starProjection_ker_isotone : ∀ {H₁ H₂ W₁ W₂ : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H₁]
  [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₁] [CompleteSpace H₁] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup H₂]
  [inst_4 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₂] [inst_5 : CompleteSpace H₂] [inst_6 : NormedAddCommGroup W₁]
  [inst_7 : NormedSpace ℂ W₁] [inst_8 : NormedAddCommGroup W₂] [inst_9 : NormedSpace ℂ W₂] (ι : H₁ →ₗᵢ[ℂ] H₂)
  (D₁ : H₁ →L[ℂ] W₁) (D₂ : H₂ →L[ℂ] W₂) (V : W₁ →ₗᵢ[ℂ] W₂),
  (∀ (x : H₁), D₂ (ι x) = V (D₁ x)) → ∀ {x : H₁}, x ∈ (↑D₁).ker → (↑D₂).ker.starProjection (ι x) = ι x
@TGLExt.lagrangian_zero_iff_mem_ker : ∀ {H₁ W₁ : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H₁] [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H₁]
  [CompleteSpace H₁] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup W₁] [inst_4 : NormedSpace ℂ W₁] (D : H₁ →L[ℂ] W₁) (x : H₁),
  ‖D x‖ = 0 ↔ x ∈ (↑D).ker
TGLExt.HilbertHomeData : (Region : Type) →
  (Region → Region → Prop) →
    (H W : Region → Type) →
      [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] →
        [(O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)] →
          [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)] → [(O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] → Type 1
@TGLExt.HilbertHomeData.PF : {Region : Type} →
  {leR : Region → Region → Prop} →
    {H W : Region → Type} →
      [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] →
        [inst_1 : (O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)] →
          [∀ (O : Region), CompleteSpace (H O)] →
            [inst_3 : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)] →
              [inst_4 : (O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] →
                TGLExt.HilbertHomeData Region leR H W → (O : Region) → H O →L[ℂ] H O
@TGLExt.HilbertHomeData.PF_internal_fix : ∀ {Region : Type} {leR : Region → Region → Prop} {H W : Region → Type}
  [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] [inst_1 : (O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)]
  [inst_2 : ∀ (O : Region), CompleteSpace (H O)] [inst_3 : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)]
  [inst_4 : (O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] (P : TGLExt.HilbertHomeData Region leR H W) (O : Region) (s : ℝ)
  (x : H O), (P.PF O) ((P.internal O s) x) = (P.internal O s) ((P.PF O) x)
@TGLExt.HilbertHomeData.PF_external_covariant : ∀ {Region : Type} {leR : Region → Region → Prop} {H W : Region → Type}
  [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] [inst_1 : (O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)]
  [inst_2 : ∀ (O : Region), CompleteSpace (H O)] [inst_3 : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)]
  [inst_4 : (O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] (P : TGLExt.HilbertHomeData Region leR H W) (g : P.G) (O : Region)
  (y : H (P.act g O)), (P.PF (P.act g O)) y = (P.external g O) ((P.PF O) ((P.external g O).symm y))
@TGLExt.HilbertHomeData.PF_isotone : ∀ {Region : Type} {leR : Region → Region → Prop} {H W : Region → Type}
  [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] [inst_1 : (O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)]
  [inst_2 : ∀ (O : Region), CompleteSpace (H O)] [inst_3 : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)]
  [inst_4 : (O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] (P : TGLExt.HilbertHomeData Region leR H W) {O₁ O₂ : Region}
  (hle : leR O₁ O₂) {x : H O₁}, x ∈ (↑(P.locks O₁)).ker → (P.PF O₂) ((P.incl hle) x) = (P.incl hle) x
@TGLExt.BreuerTraceData : {Region : Type} →
  {leR : Region → Region → Prop} →
    {H W : Region → Type} →
      [inst : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (H O)] →
        [inst_1 : (O : Region) → InnerProductSpace ℂ (H O)] →
          [∀ (O : Region), CompleteSpace (H O)] →
            [inst_3 : (O : Region) → NormedAddCommGroup (W O)] →
              [inst_4 : (O : Region) → NormedSpace ℂ (W O)] → TGLExt.HilbertHomeData Region leR H W → Type
@TGLExt.solder_recovers_curvature : ∀ {A B : Type} [inst : AddCommGroup A] [inst_1 : AddCommGroup B]
  [inst_2 : Module ℂ A] [inst_3 : Module ℂ B] (ρ : A →ₗ[ℂ] B),
  Function.Injective ⇑ρ → ∀ {F : B}, F ∈ ρ.range → ∃! R, ρ R = F

TGLExt.rhoTwo : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ
TGLExt.rhoOne_posDef : TGLExt.rhoOne.PosDef
TGLExt.rhoTwo_posDef : TGLExt.rhoTwo.PosDef
TGLExt.rhoOne_trace : TGLExt.rhoOne.trace = 1
TGLExt.rhoTwo_trace : TGLExt.rhoTwo.trace = 1
TGLExt.both_homes_exist : Nonempty (TGLExt.FiniteNameGNS TGLExt.rhoOne) ∧ Nonempty (TGLExt.FiniteNameGNS TGLExt.rhoTwo)
TGLExt.omega_one_underdetermines_home : TGLExt.gibbs TGLExt.rhoOne 1 = 1 ∧
  TGLExt.gibbs TGLExt.rhoTwo 1 = 1 ∧
    Module.finrank ℂ (Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) ℂ) ≠ Module.finrank ℂ (Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ)
TGLExt.PsiHomeData : Type → (n : Type) → [Fintype n] → [DecidableEq n] → Type

  [inst : Fintype n] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] → TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n → Region → Matrix n n ℂ → ℂ
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.name_one : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]
  (Ψ : TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n) (O : Region), Ψ.name O 1 = 1
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.home : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]
  (Ψ : TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n) (O : Region), TGLExt.FiniteNameGNS (Ψ.rho O)
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.flow : {Region n : Type} →
  [inst : Fintype n] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] → TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n → Region → ℝ → Matrix n n ℂ → Matrix n n ℂ
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.name_flow_invariant : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]
  (Ψ : TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n) (O : Region) (t : ℝ) (a : Matrix n n ℂ), Ψ.name O (Ψ.flow O t a) = Ψ.name O a
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.flow_comp : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]
  (Ψ : TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n) (O : Region) (s t : ℝ) (a : Matrix n n ℂ),
  Ψ.flow O s (Ψ.flow O t a) = Ψ.flow O (s + t) a
@TGLExt.PsiHomeData.flow_fixes_spectral_corner : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]
  (Ψ : TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n) (O : Region) {Hm : Matrix n n ℂ},
  Commute (Ψ.rho O) Hm → ∀ (f : ℝ → ℝ) (t : ℝ), Ψ.flow O t (cfc f Hm) = cfc f Hm

  Nonempty (TGLExt.PsiHomeData Region n)
@TGLExt.absoluteOne_name_eq_trace : ∀ {Region n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n]

  (A B C : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.excite A 1 = 0 ∧ TGLExt.excite B 1 = 0 ∧ TGLExt.excite C 1 = 0
@TGLExt.commutator_kernel_inhabited : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] [Nonempty n]
  (A : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.excite A 1 = 0 ∧ 1 ≠ 0
@TGLExt.corner_fixes_inhabitant : ∀ {H W : Type} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup H] [inst_1 : InnerProductSpace ℂ H]
  [inst_2 : CompleteSpace H] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup W] [inst_4 : NormedSpace ℂ W] (D : H →L[ℂ] W) {u : H},
  D u = 0 → (↑D).ker.starProjection u = u
@TGLExt.modularGen : {n : Type} → [Fintype n] → [DecidableEq n] → Matrix n n ℂ → Matrix n n ℂ → Matrix n n ℂ
@TGLExt.modularGen_eq_neg_excite : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (ρ y : Matrix n n ℂ),
  TGLExt.modularGen ρ y = -TGLExt.excite (TGLExt.logRho ρ) y
@TGLExt.modularGen_omega_zero : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (ρ : Matrix n n ℂ),
  ρ.PosDef → TGLExt.modularGen ρ (TGLExt.Omega ρ) = 0
@TGLExt.J_modularGen_J : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (ρ y : Matrix n n ℂ),
  TGLExt.Jconj (TGLExt.modularGen ρ (TGLExt.Jconj y)) = -TGLExt.modularGen ρ y
@TGLExt.parity_fixed_eq_zero : ∀ {n : Type} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] {y : Matrix n n ℂ}, y = -y → y = 0
@TGLExt.absolute_modularGen_zero : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] [Nonempty n]
  (y : Matrix n n ℂ), TGLExt.modularGen (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) y = 0
@TGLExt.absolute_faces_half : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] [Nonempty n] {Γ : Matrix n n ℂ},
  Γ.trace = 0 →
    TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 + Γ)) = 1 / 2 ∧
      TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 - Γ)) = 1 / 2
@TGLExt.absolute_contrast_zero : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] [Nonempty n]
  {Γ : Matrix n n ℂ},
  Γ.trace = 0 →
    TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 - Γ)) - TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 + Γ)) = 0
TGLExt.qKappa : ℝ → ℝ
TGLExt.alphaKappa : ℝ → ℝ
TGLExt.one_eq_q_sq_add_alpha_sq : ∀ (κ : ℝ), TGLExt.qKappa κ ^ 2 + TGLExt.alphaKappa κ ^ 2 = 1
TGLExt.q_odd : ∀ (κ : ℝ), TGLExt.qKappa (-κ) = -TGLExt.qKappa κ
TGLExt.alpha_even : ∀ (κ : ℝ), TGLExt.alphaKappa (-κ) = TGLExt.alphaKappa κ
TGLExt.q_zero : TGLExt.qKappa 0 = 0
TGLExt.alpha_zero : TGLExt.alphaKappa 0 = 1
TGLExt.cosh_half_hasDerivAt : ∀ (κ : ℝ), HasDerivAt (fun u => Real.cosh (u / 2)) (Real.sinh (κ / 2) / 2) κ
TGLExt.sinh_half_hasDerivAt : ∀ (κ : ℝ), HasDerivAt (fun u => Real.sinh (u / 2)) (Real.cosh (κ / 2) / 2) κ
TGLExt.alpha_transport : ∀ (κ : ℝ), HasDerivAt TGLExt.alphaKappa (-(TGLExt.qKappa κ / 2) * TGLExt.alphaKappa κ) κ
TGLExt.alpha_deriv_zero : HasDerivAt TGLExt.alphaKappa 0 0
TGLExt.W_hasDerivAt : ∀ (κ : ℝ), HasDerivAt (fun u => TGLExt.qKappa u / 2) (TGLExt.alphaKappa κ ^ 2 / 4) κ
TGLExt.susy_threshold : ∀ (κ : ℝ), (TGLExt.qKappa κ / 2) ^ 2 + TGLExt.alphaKappa κ ^ 2 / 4 = 1 / 4
TGLExt.susy_partner_gap : ∀ (κ : ℝ),
  (TGLExt.qKappa κ / 2) ^ 2 - TGLExt.alphaKappa κ ^ 2 / 4 = 1 / 4 - TGLExt.alphaKappa κ ^ 2 / 2

TGLExt.minimal_curvature : ∀ (c₁ c₂ : ℝ),
  TGLExt.curv2 (c₁ • TGLExt.polPlus) (c₂ • TGLExt.polCross) = (2 * c₁ * c₂) • TGLExt.rotGen
@TGLExt.minimal_curvature_ne_zero : ∀ {c₁ c₂ : ℝ},
  c₁ * c₂ ≠ 0 → TGLExt.curv2 (c₁ • TGLExt.polPlus) (c₂ • TGLExt.polCross) ≠ 0
TGLExt.curvature_flat_same : ∀ (c₁ c₂ : ℝ) (A : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ), TGLExt.curv2 (c₁ • A) (c₂ • A) = 0
TGLExt.solderMetric : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ → Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ
TGLExt.solderMetric_symm : ∀ (e : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ), (TGLExt.solderMetric e).transpose = TGLExt.solderMetric e
TGLExt.solderMetric_det : ∀ (e : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ), (TGLExt.solderMetric e).det = -e.det ^ 2
@TGLExt.solder_lorentzian : ∀ {e : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ}, e.det ≠ 0 → (TGLExt.solderMetric e).det < 0
TGLExt.helicityRep : ℝ →ₗ[ℝ] Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℝ

  ∃! R, TGLExt.helicityRep R = TGLExt.curv2 (c₁ • TGLExt.polPlus) (c₂ • TGLExt.polCross)
@TGLExt.FullStaticWitness : {M : Type} → (ℝ → M → M) → Prop
@TGLExt.leakage_strictly_loses : ∀ {t β g : ℝ}, 0 < t → 0 < β → 0 < g → Real.exp (-(t * β * g)) < 1
TGLExt.full_closure_iff_flat : ∀ (t β g : ℝ), Real.exp (-(t * β * g)) = 1 ↔ t * β * g = 0
@TGLExt.beta_forbids_full_static_witness : ∀ {β g : ℝ},
  0 < β → 0 < g → ¬TGLExt.FullStaticWitness fun t x => Real.exp (-(t * β * g)) * x
@TGLExt.verb_not_identity : ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] (d : n → ℝ) (i₀ : n),
  0 < d i₀ → ∀ {t β : ℝ}, 0 < t → 0 < β → NormedSpace.exp (-(t * β) • Matrix.diagonal d) ≠ 1
@TGLExt.leakage_rate_unique : ∀ {b₁ b₂ : ℝ}, (∀ (t : ℝ), Real.exp (-(t * b₁)) = Real.exp (-(t * b₂))) → b₁ = b₂
@TGLExt.canonical_witness_is_not_full : ∀ {β g : ℝ},
  0 < β →
    0 < g →
      ∀ {n : Type} [inst : Fintype n] [inst_1 : DecidableEq n] [Nonempty n] {Γ : Matrix n n ℂ},
        Γ.trace = 0 →
          (¬TGLExt.FullStaticWitness fun t x => Real.exp (-(t * β * g)) * x) ∧
            TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 + Γ)) = 1 / 2 ∧
              TGLExt.gibbs (TGLExt.absoluteRho n) (2⁻¹ • (1 - Γ)) = 1 / 2

    0

  (0 < (TGLExt.semifiniteDimTrace ℂ TGLExt.ellTwo).tau (↑TGLExt.eraseFirst).ker ∧

    (TGLExt.semifiniteDimTrace ℂ TGLExt.ellTwo).tau (↑TGLExt.eraseFirst).kerᗮ = ⊤

  Differentiable ℝ q →
    Differentiable ℝ (deriv q) →
      (∀ (t : ℝ), q t ≠ 0) → ∀ (s : ℝ), TGLExt.ansatzRiemann1001 q s = -(q s * deriv (deriv q) s)

  Differentiable ℝ q →

      (∀ (t : ℝ), q t ≠ 0) → ∀ (s : ℝ), TGLExt.ansatzRicci00 q s = -TGLExt.ansatzRiemann1001 q s

      (∀ (t : ℝ), q t ≠ 0) → ∀ (s : ℝ), TGLExt.ansatzRicci11 q s = TGLExt.ansatzRiemann1001 q s / q s ^ 2
